  - **binary roll**: sha `4ccebebe…` → `990ee15d…` (size 529,743,552 → 703,854,720 — a ~174 MB jump), so this snapshot re-extracts everything against the new build; most binary-tree churn below is the new build's re-slicing and re-homing, not hand edits.
  - **gumroad_mcp**: a new connector — Gumroad digital products and memberships via `/opt/hatch/bin/gumroad-mcp` against Gumroad's hosted MCP service (`status` / `authorize-url` to connect, `list-tools` before the first operation, `call-tool` thereafter); reads run without asking (`get_account`, `list_products`, `get_product`, `list_sales` — the last gated on the `view_sales` scope — and `list_payouts`), changes ask (`create_draft_product`, `publish_product`, `unpublish_product`, with the product's name, price and description shown before publishing), and a failed or timed-out change is never repeated automatically because Gumroad may have completed it. The `skills/gumroad-mcp` link is also re-pointed from the absolute `/opt/hatch/connectors/gumroad_mcp` to the relative `../connectors/gumroad_mcp`.
  - **readwise_mcp**: a new connector — Readwise highlights and Reader saved articles/documents via `/opt/hatch/bin/readwise-mcp` (hosted MCP at `mcp2.readwise.io`, OAuth scope `openid read write`); reads (highlights, daily review, Reader documents and details, tags, document highlights, export status) run without asking, every change asks for fresh approval (new highlights/documents, bulk metadata edits, exports), exports run as jobs followed with `reader_get_export_documents_status`, and the user's own notes are kept apart from quoted text. The `skills/readwise-mcp` link is likewise re-pointed to `../connectors/readwise_mcp`.
  - **runway_mcp**: a new connector — Runway video, image and audio generation and saved workflows via `/opt/hatch/bin/runway-mcp`; reads run without asking (`whoami`, `list_workspaces`, `show_plans_and_credits`, `list_recent`, `get_task`, `list_workflows`, `get_workflow`), every media call asks on every call (video/image/audio generation and editing, background removal, upscaling, HDR conversion, `localize_ad`, workflow runs and uploads — generation spends credits, so check `show_plans_and_credits` when a request may exceed the balance), workflow editing (`create_workflow`, `save_workflow_version`, `validate_workflow_graph`) is a separate ask-every-time permission, and a failed or timed-out generation is never repeated before checking `get_task` / `list_recent`. The `skills/runway-mcp` link is likewise re-pointed to `../connectors/runway_mcp`.
  - **vidiq_mcp**: a new connector — vidIQ YouTube/Instagram/TikTok research via `/opt/hatch/bin/vidiq-mcp`; research runs without asking (own-channel analytics, performance trends and subscriber insights, channel/video lookup, transcripts and comments, YouTube/channel search, similar channels/videos/thumbnails, outliers, trending, keyword research, Instagram/TikTok outlier search and profiles, competitors, title/thumbnail scoring, credit balance and job polling), while generation and analysis ask on every call and can consume credits (`vidiq_generate_titles`, `vidiq_generate_script`, `vidiq_generate_thumbnail`, `vidiq_refine_thumbnail`, `vidiq_video_watch`, `vidiq_watch_shortform_content`, `vidiq_update_competitors`); the connector cannot edit, upload or publish YouTube videos, own-channel analytics are kept separate from trend research and generated suggestions, and long jobs are polled before reporting success. The `skills/vidiq-mcp` link is likewise re-pointed to `../connectors/vidiq-mcp`.
  - **duffel**: the booking reference is reworked around Stripe Link prefill — when the user picks an offer, call `wallet.list_payment_methods` (`provider: "stripe-link"`) and `wallet.get_user_info` in the same turn, prefill the trip email and phone from the conversation, `~/USER.md` and `wallet.get_user_info`, use the user's own name for a passenger only when the conversation shows the user is flying, and show the prefilled name, email and phone in the question so the user can correct a name that differs from their ID; then ask in one message for what is still missing (usually birth dates, genders and unknown legal names — if Link is `not_connected`, end that message with Link's connection link unchanged so the user connects while answering); the CLI now sets `title` from gender (the example passenger loses its `title` field) and gives every passenger the first passenger's email and phone, the first-time payment profile falls back to `wallet.get_user_info` for the account holder's email and legal name, a Link connection that lands before the traveler details gets a one-line confirmation and then waits, and traveler details stay out of replies apart from the prefilled name, email and phone shown for confirmation.
  - **facebook_cli**: `friends.md` — `--birthday-within-days` now accepts `0` for today (up to 365, default 7), and for today's birthdays you pass `0` and keep only friends whose `birthday_date` is today, because the results can also include tomorrow's; `saved.md` — each saved item's `collection_ids` now lists every collection that holds it by the IDs `saved collections list` returns (empty means no collection, and it lists all of them even when `--collection-id` narrows the read), `saved collections list` gains `--limit`/`--after` paging (a page holds at most 20 collections; read every page before creating a collection or choosing among them, stopping once a named collection appears), and for both commands a missing `paging` means there are no more results.
  - **agoda**: the connector doc gains Place Discovery and Trip Planning as related skills; **geo** and **place-discovery** (both copies) and **travel-planning** / **trip-planning** (both copies) each gain Agoda as a related connector "for finding things to do at the destination, including tours, attraction tickets and experiences."
  - **muse_mail**: the manifest's availability scopes list (`internal-test, prod, quail, canary, alpha, swarm`) is removed from `connector.yaml`, leaving only `confidential_vm: false`.
  - **opentable**: the manifest maps two more commands — `cancel-payment-setup: null` (with the comment that Link cleanup uses owner-scoped checkout fences, not reservation write consent) and `recover-reservation: availability.read`.
  - **shopping** (both the `skills/` and `workflow-skills/` copies): three presentation rules are added — when talking to the user, never name the catalog, the searches, browser tasks or subagents or describe what they are doing (describe the work in the first person as your own); a status or "why is it taking a while" question is answered as your own effort in the first person; and successful shopping means communicating like someone shopping on the user's behalf, free of technical jargon, with specs in the user's words, never as SKUs, IDs or raw field values.
  - **binary-etc — docs/approvals**: the browser `page.upload_files` approval policy changes from `fresh_consent_only` to `fresh_consent_or_site_allow` (label "Upload files", phrase "upload this file using Browser").
  - **system_prompts — shopping handoff**: `turn_directives/shopping_handoff.md` is rewritten (complete the usual memory lookup first, then send one brief acknowledgment in public commentary followed by `subagent.spawn` in the same response, mentioning earlier work only when memory or history supports it), and three new extracts split the flow further — `shopping_handoff_after_spawn.md` (memory lookup, spawn, then acknowledge in a normal response and end the turn, since commentary is not available for that acknowledgment), `shopping_handoff_commentary.md` (the one written commentary acknowledgment replaces the reaction-only task acknowledgment and itself confirms delegation, with no other commentary) and `shopping_handoff_continuation.md` (the runtime may end the turn after the acknowledgment and deliver the subagent's results later; an already-visible acknowledgment is not repeated, and when a subagent reports back the main agent continues the task with its own tools).
  - **system_prompts — background / chat / artifacts**: `background/execute_resolve.md` drops out and `background/objective_step.md` is re-sliced to a blank extract; `chat/date_time_awareness_head.md` now templates the time-tag placement as `{time_tag_placement}` and a stub `chat/time_tag_after_input.md` appears; the artifacts `web_builder_request_context.md` scope rule is tightened — the verbatim build request and an accepted assistant proposal define the scope, and other parent-conversation facts now shape how you build that scope (the clause that sample or demo records still need the user's own ask under Content Integrity is gone); spaces_builder_lite's `edit_who_you_are.md` now says a requested new style beyond colors is carried through layout, typography and components, and its two verify-steps extracts are re-sliced.
  - **browser site-config**: two new schema-2 extracts appear — `chewy.com` (covering chewy.com and petmd.com, a `mid-auth-chewy` header and `X-Personal-Agent-ID` sourced from `hbi_release_marker`, both marked public diagnostics) and `toasttab.com` (covering toasttab.com and toast.app, the same shape with a `mid-auth-toast` header).
  - **bot_challenge**: `bot_challenge.guide.json` is identified again as a named tool (and returns to the bot_challenge topic index, now 4 tools) — it asks the runtime to reload the page or move the tab to a fresh browser (`reload_page` / `replace_environment`, one move attempt per task); `browser.pay.json` drops out and the named `browser.login`, `browser.request_input`, `browser.steer_specialist` and `browser.kill_specialist` tool JSONs are de-identified back to unidentified files at their new offsets.

the shopping profile pipeline instead reappears under `binary-skills/shopping/` as five 1-line blank extracts (`profile.md`, `system/core.md`, `system/pipeline.md`, `system/steps/judge.md`, `system/steps/profile.md`) while the old fused `binary-skills/shopping/PROFILE.md` drops out;   - **live-page-checks**: a new workflow skill, "Price and Stock Checks" — read the store's own product page first with `browser.open` and a `max_age_minutes` budget (about 15 for a one-time check, the watch's interval and at least 30 for a scheduled watch, whose job body must save the page URL and state that budget), answer from the page text when it states the price or stock (the `## Product Offer` block loses to the page's own buy-button/stock text on a disagreement), and fall back to a live `browser.browse` task only when the read fails, is a robot/sign-in/error page or the wrong product, or the answer needs a variant, location, cart or member price the page text does not settle; a failed read never means out of stock.
  - **style-tryon**: the try-on selection in `references/flow.md` changes — if the user names products, try on just those in their order; otherwise try on the 2 products named in the reply with the results (filling from the next results of the requested kind, catalog first, when one cannot be tried on), with no three-item cap; render once per selected product and name any that were not rendered, and the "which do you like best" options row now appears whenever two or more were shown; the photo rule's reason is reworded to "only use photos the user uploaded specifically for try-on".
  - **browser specialists / checkout**: `browser.pay` is now used only when the task calls for a purchase (not to prepare a cart), the browser agent no longer makes cart changes itself — it leaves cart preparation and changes to the checkout agent, steering a working specialist or re-calling `browser.pay` with the same checkout reference and the requested changes — and the checkout agent's own workflow is rewritten to always record the cart with `checkout.prepare` at `status: draft` first, get Muse's review, then re-prepare at `status: ready` (a `completed` report now requires `outcome: placed`); a new `bot_challenge.guide.json` tool appears, asking the runtime to reload the page or move the tab to a fresh browser (`reload_page` / `replace_environment`, one move attempt per task).
  - **system_prompts — spaces_builder**: the Muse template's `static_color_rules.md` expands from a one-liner to the long version — keep the starter `<head>` theme script, `hatch:space:theme` listener and semantic-role/palette `<style>` block, style with the roles and palette variables (never hex, both modes via `light-dark()`, never `prefers-color-scheme`), the `muse-typography` ramp rules, and the accent, safe-area, app-title and Muse-icon rules.
  - **binary-skills — chat-connections / voice / session**: `chat-connections/muse.md` appears (374 lines), a new `voice/raw_context_identity_guidance.md` appears (519 lines — the voice session's derived-context summarizer that compacts USER.md and MEMORY.md into one "User Identity and Memory" section of at most 2,000 tokens, keeping durable identity, relationships and preferences and dropping stale snapshots, credentials and incidental detail), `session/steering_handoff_worker_result.md` appears (274 lines), and the voice overrides each gain a chain line. (+3428/−2148, 55 files)

  - **duffel**: the connector now requires confirming Duffel is returned by `muse.connector_search` before running any Duffel command, including `--help` — if absent, use Flight Booking's browser fallback and do not create a Duffel fare job; two stop rules are spelled out: `connector_country_not_allowed` stops all Duffel commands for the task, and `duffel_unavailable` stops search/places/seat-options/validate-booking/book for the rest of the turn.
  - **duffel — fare-check**: the daily fare check now checks `muse.connector_search` before every run, makes no further Duffel calls after a country-not-allowed or unavailable result, and treats a skipped or refused search as a failed read that keeps the job and any pending candidate with a failure note.
  - **flight-booking**: Duffel moves under "When available"; the skill now requires `muse.connector_search` to find available flight connectors before reading their docs or running their CLIs, goes directly to Google Flights or the airline's site when none is available, and its description and flights reference are reworded to match ("search available connected flight inventory first").
  - **flight-tracking**: the fare job becomes optional — it requires Duffel to be returned by `muse.connector_search` before its docs are read or any calls scheduled, status monitoring continues without it with the fare-coverage limitation recorded, and the skill now speaks of "the trip's monitoring jobs" and "the ids of the jobs that exist" instead of always two jobs; the binary-extracted reconcile step is rewritten the same way (status-only monitoring counts as complete when the fare job is ineligible).
  - **meta_catalog_search**: catalog results no longer carry the `is_agentic_checkout_creation_enabled` / `is_agentic_checkout_completion_enabled` flags in the doc, and purchase routing now runs on product details, not checkout flags — a product whose details carry a `checkout_token` uses Shopify UCP checkout whatever its flags say.
  - **shopify-ucp**: eligibility switches from the agentic-checkout flags to the product-details `checkout_token` throughout — the connector and its checkout protocol now take each product as its `checkout_token` in cart and checkout items (a saved cart line is passed as `item_id` with no token), ignore the checkout flags, retry a `no_ucp_descriptor` failure once with a fresh token from product-details, complete with Shop Pay or Link without the old completion-flag gates, and treat an `escalation.continue_url` from `checkout complete` as "no order placed — finish in the browser with the same wallet and card, paying there if asked" instead of checking `order get`.
  - **shopping**: both copies of the Shopping skill mirror the checkout-token switch — products with a `checkout_token` keep their merchant cart under Shopify UCP, and the Shopify UCP link is now described as checkout for catalog products that carry a `checkout_token`.
  - **moneyforward_me**: manifest labels are reworded only — the read action becomes "Read income, expense, asset and liability data", `assets.read` becomes "Read account balances and the value of held securities", and `transactions.read` becomes "Read transaction details, income and expense by category" (approval phrases updated to match).
  - **system_prompts — peer_chat**: `evidence.md` now says to check a concern against its evidence before repeating it (the other Muse's concern is not additional evidence) and to keep urgency proportionate; `private_sidebars.md` says an optional question from the other Muse can stay open rather than interrupting the user for background; `proactivity.md` says to close out work the user says they have handled and not let a peer suggestion reopen closed work; `turn_collaboration.md` and `writing.md` now apply Writing Style and Final Response Brevity to `mailbox.send_message` and `owner.send_message` text as well.   - **meta-ads**: the connector doc is restructured — when several accounts are reachable and the user names none, ask which they mean before reading figures (a compare/all-accounts request is answered per account instead); a single-figure answer still names the metric, object and period; a new performance-overview passage says to choose metrics against the campaign's goal (CPM for delivery cost, Purchase ROAS for purchase campaigns), with advertiser-requested metrics taking priority and unavailable figures left explicit; the task-guide list now routes creative ideas to campaign-creative, new campaigns to campaign creation, other changes to write operations, and manual setup to its own triggers (write-disabled account, a write rejected as unavailable, or the advertiser building it themselves); and generated creative must be linked directly by its returned file location.   - **meta-ads — campaign-creative**: the reference is rewritten around four stages — exploring ideas, planning the ad, preparing the creative and reviewing it — starting where the advertiser is and not taking them further than they asked; the plan now covers what people will see, format and copy, where it runs, the chosen or open source options and any open choices, is built from the accepted strategy and verified product details (public ads inspire but prove nothing), and accepting the plan approves only the chosen preparation, never campaign creation or publishing; the old required best-ads grounding step, source-action options widget and `ads_create_creative` schema pre-read are gone, and the review gate's revision option is renamed "Tell me what to change".

## 2026-10-21
- [`e1ab875`](https://github.com/sneakers-the-rat/muse-skills/commit/e1ab87585118553b22f1ce04745148c8011f4041) 23:02
  - **binary roll**: the hatch binary changed (binary-map.json sha256 `183ed958…` → `ab148199…`), so this snapshot re-extracts everything against the new build — binary-map.json's regenerated manifest (new offsets and targets) accounts for most of its churn, and most binary-tree churn below is the new build's content landing at shifted offsets, not hand edits.
  - **muse-feedback** (skills/ and workflow-skills/ copies): the `file --client-surface` mapping now also maps `Instagram app` to `instagram` and `Messenger app` to `messenger`, alongside the existing web/ios/android and provider-channel values.
  - **muse_db** (skills/ and workflow-skills/ copies): the `runtime.browser_tasks` table reference documents a new column — `last_progress_at`, `timestamp with time zone`, required, default `now()`.
  - **docs — calls-texts-notifications**: the phone voice-choice flow is rewritten — a first-time calling question now means check `phone.begin_call` and follow its setup instructions before describing the account's callers; Hailey is the female voice (she/her) and Brett the male voice (he/him); after AI is selected the precedence is an explicit voice choice (a request by name counts), then the returned saved or default voice, which needs no further choice; `voice_status=choice_required` means ask which voice and wait, `deferred` means follow setup until `phone.schedule_call` resolves the slot, `not_required` means omit voice from confirmation; voices are introduced or a test offered only when returned setup instructs it, and the test-versus-business question is shown and waited on without skip/decline language; "Scheduled calls replay" becomes "Frozen schedules replay their approved arguments."

  - **binary-skills — voice**: heuristic re-slicing under the new build redraws the voice files — `voice/action.md` (the `set_requested_voice` fast-action guidance) is deleted and its text appears nowhere else in the new tree; `voice/raw_context_identity_guidance.md` grows from 1 to 520 lines as the full Avatar Prompt Compiler prompt (both the no-source-image and reference-image modes, with species matrix, faithful/diverse rules, and guardrails) is extracted into it for the first time in the archive; `voice/screenless/visible_text_routing_companion_capture.md` shrinks from 762 lines to its single routing paragraph as the fused browser-tool text leaves it; `voice/overrides/app_audio_voice_chat.md` and `app_voice_chat.md` become one-line pointer stubs naming the next override file.

  - **binary-strings/artifacts**: the fused duplicate `…/artifacts/shared/images.md` is deleted (its canonical copy already exists at `system_prompts/artifacts/shared/images.md`), and `artifacts/system/web/builder/workflow/build_step_exit_driven.md` is deleted — the `web_artifacts.exit_build` step text appears nowhere else in the new tree.

  - **code — extract_binary_files.py**: the immediate-store finder is rewritten — the naive `IMM8_RE`/`IMM32_RE` regexes (which misread rsp-based stores, `48 c7 44 24 ..`) are replaced by a module-level `imm_stores()` that properly decodes ModRM/SIB and displacement bytes and returns only `movq $imm32` (/0) stores, which are exactly the stack-built descriptors the regex missed.   - **code — extract_binary_files.py**: the file extractor becomes a one-pass deterministic merger with ranked authority — (1) `(path, data)` relocation tables (R_X86_64_RELATIVE fat-pointer slices, path slice with its data slice at +16), (2) content slices whose length replaces a heuristic length when a heuristic file's content address is a slice target, (3) struct-store constructor descriptors (LEA path/store/immediate lengths/LEA content), (4) register-length constructor descriptors (only adjacency-verified or after-the-LEA picks, with an overlap guard rejecting any span that swallows another file's start), and (5) the old heuristic string-splitting, now computed first but used only for files no higher mechanism covers. A file covered by a higher mechanism is never taken from a lower one; the run now prints counts by mechanism, new/changed-on-disk counts and the heuristic-only residue, only writes files that are new or changed, and fails loudly only when neither bundle clusters nor tables are found. The manifest format is unchanged ({source, target, offset}).
  - **binary-map.json / binary roll**: the archived binary itself rolled — SHA-256 `45b15cc2…` → `183ed958…`, size 506,822,688 → 508,260,288 bytes — so most offsets in the manifest re-base, and the manifest also gains a fused-path source entry (`artifacts/markdownartifacts/spreadsheetartifacts/…/artifacts/shared/images.md`, artifact image/project-folder guidance) alongside the cleanly named `system-prompt/artifacts/shared/images.md`. Because the deterministic extractor and the binary roll land together, the mass binary-tree churn below is extraction re-slicing plus new-binary content, not 155 separate hand edits.

  - **shopify_ucp**: the connector doc now says to read the checkout protocol also before checking an order placed through native checkout. `checkout-protocol.md` scopes the protocol to native orders confirmed in-conversation by a successful `checkout complete` or a confirming `order get`; after a successful completion it now requires following the new **Check order status** section — read the placed order via `order get --checkout-id` with the saved `agent_state.checkout_id`, check top-level `ok` first, include one verbatim `[View order](<order_url>)` link by default (merchant order page preferred, then confirmation, then status URL; never invent/shorten a link or expose IDs), prefer `fulfillment.events[].tracking_url` for tracking, relay refund/return/credit/dispute messages — and if that read fails the completion still stands and checkout must never be repeated to recover details or a link; browser-completed Shop Pay/Link orders stay on the browser-owned path.
  - **muse_db** (in both `skills/` and `workflow-skills/`): the `runtime.checkout_spend_operations` table doc gains two nullable telemetry columns — `original_request_trace_json` (`text`; original request attribution captured at operation begin, never replaced by retries/recovery, NULL = not recorded) and `checkout_attribution` (`jsonb`; optional original checkout provider and normalized merchant origin, immutable after begin, NULL for predecessor rows/writers, explicitly not authorization or replay identity).
  - **financial-planning** (workflow-skills): the connector list adds Money Forward ME for balances, asset holdings, transactions and household spending by category.
  - **binary-skills / system-prompt / self_improvement / browser / artifacts — extraction re-slicing**: deterministic lengths cut trailing fused material the heuristic had appended to many files (e.g. `binary-skills/session/avatar_completion_handoff.md` drops 665 lines of fused unrelated costume/avatar strings, `binary-skills/voice/calling_device_tools.md` drops 460, `binary-skills/session/connectors.md` and `chat/scheduling-and-watching.md` lose fused trailing fragments, `browser/workflows/checkout/system.md` loses 62 trailing lines) and complete files the heuristic had truncated (e.g. `self_improvement/studying/study_note.md` gains 240 lines: the completed avatar-state path plus Do-not-edit list, goal-tool helpers, voice/register and the full study process including the ban on learning-style matching; `self_improvement/memory/system/steps/reconcile.md` and `self_improvement/shopping/profile.md` are likewise re-cut).   - **shopping**: the pre-search questioning rules are rewritten under a new "Decide whether to ask before searching" section (in both the skills and workflow-skills copies). The shopping profile is now read at the start of every request, before deciding whether to ask anything, and the profile or memory is never referred to in replies or questions unless the user asks — a stored fact that explains a pick is stated as the fact itself. Search now runs without asking when product type, required attributes and relevant known preferences are enough, and missing recipient or acceptable-condition information is not a separate prerequisite unless it is a required attribute or a soft preference that would improve the search. A single missing required attribute with a known answer set, or an unknown product type within a category, gets one `muse.create_options` question; several open required attributes are asked together in one plain-text message instead of one per message in sequence; soft preferences are asked in plain text only, all in one message, at most three short questions with exactly one preference per question, never with `muse.create_options`, never for budget, never alongside a product-type or required-attribute question, and never with other questions in the same message as a card. After an answer, search if a useful search is now possible, otherwise ask one more focused question; if the user skips or says go ahead, search with what is known, and for an unprovided required attribute search each likely value separately and label the results by value rather than guessing.
